### [1] [Clustering and Token Denoising for Faster and More Robust VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19285)
*Baptiste Rossigneux,Inna Kucher,Vincent Lorrain,Emmanuel Casseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ClustRS，一个两阶段的免训练token剪枝算法，结合注意力加权聚类和残差收缩去噪，在大幅减少视觉token数量的同时提升VLM对噪声图像的鲁棒性，在ScienceQA-IMG和MM-VET上相比现有方法在极端噪声下性能提升高达20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（如LLaVA）需要处理高达576或729个视觉token，计算负担严重阻碍边缘部署。现有token剪枝技术大多需要重新训练，难以适应架构变化。此外，真实世界数据常含噪声，现有剪枝方法对噪声鲁棒性不足。因此需要一个免训练、轻量且鲁棒的token剪枝方案。

Method: ClustRS包含两个核心组件：(1) 注意力加权的聚类算法，从每个语义簇中选取代表性token；(2) 残差收缩（Residual Shrinkage），一种单遍去噪步骤作用于选中的token。整个过程是训练无关的轻量级操作。

Result: 在ScienceQA-IMG和MM-VET基准上，ClustRS优于基于注意力和基于多样性的方法，在极端噪声和token条件下（token减少97%，仅剩16个token）在LLaVA 1.5 7b上性能提升高达20%；在LLaVA-OneVision上，使用不到三分之一的token即可在轻度噪声条件下匹配基线性能。

Conclusion: ClustRS证明了将基于评分和基于多样性的剪枝规则相结合（而非单一使用）是一种简单而强大的替代方案，为计算高效且噪声鲁棒的VLM边缘部署开辟了道路。

Abstract: Recent Visual-Language Models (VLMs) have enhanced the capabilities of pre-trained LLMs by adding vision tokens alongside text, with approaches like LLaVA showing impressive results. However, the computational burden of processing up to 576 or 729 visual tokens makes edge deployment challenging. While various token pruning techniques require retraining, some are training-free and thus can easily adapt to architecture changes. We introduce ClustRS, a two-part, training-free algorithm for robust token pruning. Its first component is an attention-weighted, clustering algorithm that selects representative tokens from each semantic cluster. The second component, Residual Shrinkage, is a one-pass denoising step on the selected tokens. These training-free lightweight steps make LLaVA ready for real-world data, improving robustness to a wide range of image-noise types and intensities. Experimental results on the ScienceQA-IMG and MM-VET benchmarks show our method outperforms attention- and diversity-based methods by up to 20\% under extreme noise and token conditions (reducing tokens by 97\%, down to 16 tokens) on LLaVA 1.5 7b and achieves exceptional results on LLaVA-OneVision, where we match baseline performance with fewer than one-third of their tokens under mild noise conditions. Our study demonstrates a simple yet powerful alternative to both score-only and diversity-only pruning rules, paving the way for compute-efficient and noise-resilient VLM deployment.

</details>


### [2] [SceneGTMM: A Conformal Mapping-based Scene-Aware Transferable GNN-Transformer Dual-Graph Interaction Framework for Map Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19298)
*Yongliang Zhang,Feng Song,Ji Chen,Lishuai Guo,Yong Deng,Yue Zheng,Tianyi Liu,Zhixiong Chen,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SceneGTMM，一个基于共形映射场景相对策略的可迁移GNN-Transformer双图交互地图匹配框架，通过局部坐标系构建支持跨区域迁移，结合GNN道路图与Transformer轨迹图实现局部拓扑约束与全局时序依赖融合，并通过CRF增强结构化预测，在16-50米定位误差下达到80%以上精度，跨城市迁移场景优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图匹配方法在噪声鲁棒性、跨区域迁移和可解释性方面存在不足，具体表现为局部-全局融合不充分、难以适应动态路网更新、依赖黑盒模型缺乏可解释性。

Method: 1) 共形映射场景相对策略：构建以轨迹为中心的局部坐标系，降低对训练路网的依赖，支持跨区域迁移与动态路网更新；2) GNN-Transformer双图交互架构：GNN建模道路图捕获局部拓扑约束，Transformer建模轨迹图捕获全局时序依赖，通过跨图注意力实现噪声抑制与语义对齐；3) CRF增强结构化预测：结合Transformer全局上下文与CRF拓扑转移约束，提升路径连通性与鲁棒性。

Result: 在多源轨迹、16-50米定位误差条件下，SceneGTMM实现80%以上精度，较HMM提升5.3%；在跨城市迁移场景中优于MTrajRec、GraphMM和TMM，并通过注意力与相对坐标可视化增强了可解释性。

Conclusion: 该研究为实时交通感知与自动驾驶路径规划提供了高精度、可迁移地图匹配的新范式。

Abstract: Map matching is a key technology connecting positioning data with high precision road networks, but it faces challenges in noise robustness, cross regional transfer, and interpretability. To addr ess the limitations of existing methods in local global fusion, dynamic road network adaptation, and reliance on black box mod els, this paper proposes SceneGTMM, a transferable GNN Transformer dual graph interaction map matching framework based on a conformal mapping based scene relative strategy. 1) Conformal mapping based scene relative strategy: constructs trajectory centric local coordinate systems to reduce dependence on the training road network, supporting cross regional transfer and dynamic road network updates; 2) GNN Transformer dual graph interaction architecture: a GNN modeled road graph captures local topological constraints, while a Transformer modeled trajectory graph captures global temporal dependencies, and cross graph attention achieves noise suppression and semantic alignment; 3) CRF enhanced structured prediction: combines the global context of the Transformer with the topological transition constraints of CRF to improve path connectivity and robustness. Experiments show that SceneGTM achieves over 80% accuracy on multi source trajectories with positioning errors of 16 50 meters, representing a 5.3% improvement over HMM. In cross city transfer scenarios, it outperforms MTrajRec, GraphMM, and TMM, and enhances interpretability through attention and relative coordinate visualization. This study provides a new paradigm for high precision, transferable map matching for real time traffic perception and autonomous driving path planning.

</details>


### [3] [Does Marginal Coverage Guarantee Class-Conditional Safety for Zero-Shot VLMs Under Shift?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19376)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Amartya Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了split-conformal预测在零样本视觉语言模型（VLM）部署偏移下的可靠性。研究发现，虽然边缘覆盖率保持相对较高（约0.86），但类别条件尾部覆盖率在ImageNet-Sketch上崩塌至接近0，且10-12%的类别低于有限样本零假设下限。源域诊断无法预测这一失败，源侧校准不迁移，目标侧类别校准虽有效但成本高昂，且边际覆盖率不应被视为类别尾部的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Split-conformal预测已广泛用作零样本VLM（如CLIP、OpenCLIP、SigLIP）的弃权层，但现有研究主要关注边际覆盖保证，忽视了类别条件尾部分布在部署偏移下的风险。本文旨在审计这种实践在真实部署偏移场景下的可靠性，检验边际覆盖率是否足以作为安全性保障。

Method: 研究在ImageNet及非ImageNet场景下，对CLIP、OpenCLIP和SigLIP进行系统的部署偏移审计。测试包括：边缘覆盖率与类别条件尾部覆盖率的对比分析；检验源域诊断能否预测尾部失败；评估源侧Mondrian校准、聚簇conformal和Conf-OT方法；测试目标侧类别校准；引入跨模型族效率差距分析；考察原生SigLIP sigmoid分数对APS概率质量解释的影响。变量包括模型规模、预训练语料、提示、误覆盖水平α及非ImageNet偏移设置。

Result: 边缘覆盖率可保持相对较高（约0.86），但类别条件尾部覆盖率在ImageNet-Sketch上崩塌至接近0，10-12%的类低于有限样本空假设下限。尾部失败与目标域类别准确率对齐，但源域诊断无法预测。源侧Mondrian校准仅改善分布内尾部但不迁移；聚簇conformal和Conf-OT仅改善边际或平均指标。目标侧类别校准显著提升尾部但需每类标签且集大小密集。此外发现2-3×跨族效率差距，SigLIP sigmoid分数破坏了APS的概率质量解释。所有发现跨模型规模、预训练语料、提示、误覆盖水平和非ImageNet偏移设置均一致。

Conclusion: 边际conformal覆盖率应被视为平均可靠性统计量，而非类别尾部的安全保证。现有方法在部署偏移下无法保障最差类别的覆盖；目标侧校准虽有效但成本过高。论文呼吁实践者在使用conformal作为VLM弃权层时重新审视其安全性边界。

Abstract: Split-conformal prediction provides marginal coverage under exchangeability and is increasingly used as an abstention layer for zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs). We audit this practice under deployment shift for CLIP, OpenCLIP, and SigLIP across ImageNet and non-ImageNet settings. Marginal coverage can remain relatively high while class-conditional tail coverage collapses: on ImageNet-Sketch, worst-class coverage falls to $\approx 0$ and 10-12% of classes lie below a finite-sample null floor, despite marginal coverage of about 0.86. The failure is aligned with target-domain class accuracy but is not predicted by the source-domain diagnostics we test. Source-side Mondrian calibration improves the in-distribution tail but does not transfer, while clustered conformal and Conf-OT improve marginal or average metrics without recovering the worst-class tail. Target-side class calibration substantially lifts the tail, but requires labels for every class and remains set-size-intensive. We further identify a 2-3$\times$ cross-family efficiency gap and show that native SigLIP sigmoid scores remove APS's probability-mass interpretation. The findings persist across the tested model scale, pretraining corpus, prompt, miscoverage level $α$, and shifted non-ImageNet settings. Marginal conformal coverage should therefore be treated as an average reliability statistic, not as a safety guarantee for the class tail.

</details>


### [4] [CAViAR: A Causal Video Dataset for Fine-Grained Accident Reasoning in Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19380)
*Sparsh Garg,Yi-Wen Chen,Vijay Kumar B G,Abhishek Aich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAViAR是一个包含2,249个真实行车记录仪事故视频的人类标注基准，专门评估视觉语言模型(VLM)在交通事故因果推理和责任判定方面的能力。评测发现当前VLM存在"感知-推理鸿沟"：它们虽能识别视觉上下文（如光照），但在天气、路况等感知任务及事故类型和责任归属等高级推理任务上表现不佳，甚至低于多数类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自动驾驶系统虽在感知任务（目标检测、轨迹预测）上表现出色，但缺乏解释交通事故所需的高层因果推理能力，尤其是指定事故责任方和判定违规规则方面在现有基准中几乎未被探索。

Method: 构建CAViAR基准：从CarCrashDataset和Nexar收集2,249个真实事故视频，进行人类标注，标注维度包括环境条件、事故类型、因果解释、显式责任方、受影响方和规则违反类别。使用Cosmos-Reason2、Qwen3-VL和InternVL3等先进VLM进行评测，引入多数类/随机基线和平衡指标处理类别不平衡问题。

Result: 感知能力不均衡：光照识别接近饱和，而天气和道路条件准确率低于或等于多数类基线；所有模型在事故类型和责任推理上大幅下降，展现出普遍的感知-推理鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前VLM虽能识别显著上下文信息，但无法在安全关键的驾驶场景中可靠地将可见的智能体行为映射到与规则相关的责任类别，凸显了构建更强事故因果推理能力的需求。

Abstract: While modern autonomous driving systems excel at perception tasks such as object detection and trajectory prediction, they lack the high-level causal reasoning required to interpret traffic accidents. In particular, determining responsibility, such as identifying who is at fault and which traffic rule was violated, remains largely unexplored in current benchmarks. To this end, we introduce CAViAR (Causal Accident Video and Incident Analysis Repository), a human-annotated dashcam benchmark comprising 2,249 real-world accident videos collected from CarCrashDataset (CCD) and Nexar. Each video is annotated with structured labels spanning environmental conditions, accident type, causal explanation, apparent At-Fault Agent, affected agent, and apparent rule-violation category. We benchmark state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs), including Cosmos-Reason2, Qwen3-VL, and InternVL3. Once class imbalance is accounted for with majority/random baselines and balanced metrics, perceptual competence is uneven--lighting is nearly solved, whereas weather and road-condition accuracy fall at or below the majority-class baseline---and all models degrade sharply on accident type and responsibility reasoning. Overall, CAViAR exposes a practical Perception--Reasoning Gap: current VLMs may recognize salient context, but do not reliably map visible agent actions to annotated rule-relevant responsibility categories in safety-critical driving scenarios. Code, annotation schema, prompts, and evaluation scripts are available at: https://github.com/nec-labs-ma/CAViAR

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Recognition: Compact Multi-Domain Arabic Manuscript HTR with Candidate-Selection Analysis and Evidence-Preserving Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19385)
*Abdullah Ahmed Ali,Mohammed Thamer Abdulhadi,Ali Haider Safaa,Dhulfiqar Mahdi Wadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Phoenix（一个4.99M参数的CNN-BiLSTM-CTC手写识别器）和Athar（一个基于证据感知的审校工作流），用于历史阿拉伯语手稿转录。系统通过文档感知重放、扩大的81符号编解码器和遗忘防护机制适应多个手稿领域，在预注册的评估中将两个大型保留集上的字符加权CER从19.98%降至14.93%（相对减少25.3%）。研究同时表明现有神经重排序、MBR等方法难以恢复束搜索与Oracle@25之间的2.15点差距，因此Athar以可审计的TEI/PAGE-XML记录方式保留视觉证据、暴露受限备选解读并保守使用语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史阿拉伯语手稿转录不仅是识别问题，更需要在变化的手写风格和版面布局下运作，同时保留不确定的解读、区分视觉证据与语言合理性，并记录研究者的最终决策。现有的HTR（手写文本识别）系统往往将其视为纯文本替换问题，缺乏对证据管理和可审计性的系统性支持。

Method: 提出Phoenix，一个4.99百万参数CNN-BiLSTM-CTC识别器，采用文档感知重放实现跨档案、马格里布、历史手稿领域的适应；扩大的81符号编解码器用于覆盖阿拉伯语书写变体；遗忘防护机制拒绝那些在新领域改进但严重损害先前领域性能的检查点。提出Athar审校工作流，保留视觉读取、暴露有界备选方案、保守使用局部语言模型、检索源平行文本并提供唯一/模糊/弃权状态，导出可审计的TEI和PAGE-XML记录。

Result: 在预注册的评估中，Phoenix在10,594行Agapet数据上将CER从22.12%降至17.86%，在11,684行Omar数据上从17.72%降至11.84%，在164行TariMa数据上从10.39%微升至10.72%。两个大型保留集上字符加权CER从19.98%降至14.93%（相对减少25.3%）。开发诊断为四个领域中的四个取得最低CER（9.59%未加权宏平均CER）。N-best诊断显示束解码与Oracle@25间存在2.15点差距，而神经文本重排序、共识MBR、CTC后验质量估计和局部CTC前隐蔽状态质量估计只能恢复该差距的不到4%。

Conclusion: 结果支持将手稿HTR评估视为可审计的证据管理而非静默的文本替换。Phoenix在准确性上取得了显著改进，但识别后处理技术改善空间有限，这凸显了在转录工作流中保留证据并做出可追溯决策的重要性。

Abstract: Historical Arabic manuscript transcription is not only a recognition problem. A usable scholarly system must cope with shifting hands and layouts, preserve uncertain readings, distinguish visual evidence from linguistic plausibility, and record the researcher's final decision. We present Phoenix, a 4.99-million-parameter CNN-BiLSTM-CTC recognizer, and Athar, an evidence-aware review workflow built around it. Phoenix is adapted across archival, Maghrebi, and historical manuscript domains using document-aware replay, an expanded 81-symbol codec, and forgetting guards that reject checkpoints that improve a new domain at unacceptable cost to previous domains. In a pre-specified held-out comparison against the preceding checkpoint, frozen before evaluation and scored with greedy decoding and raw references, Phoenix reduced CER from 22.12% to 17.86% on 10,594 Agapet lines and from 17.72% to 11.84% on 11,684 Omar lines, while regressing from 10.39% to 10.72% on 164 TariMa lines. Across the two large held-out sets, character-weighted CER fell from 19.98% to 14.93%, a 25.3% relative error reduction. A separate same-protocol development diagnostic found the lowest CER for Phoenix on four of four comparable domains (9.59% unweighted macro CER). An N-best diagnostic revealed a 2.15-point oracle gap between beam decoding and Oracle@25, while neural text rerankers, consensus MBR, CTC-posterior quality estimation, and local pre-CTC hidden-state quality estimation recovered less than 4% of this gap. Athar therefore preserves the visual reading, exposes bounded alternatives, uses local language models conservatively, retrieves source parallels with unique, ambiguous, or abstain states, and exports auditable TEI and PAGE-XML records. The results support evaluating manuscript HTR as auditable evidence management rather than silent text replacement.

</details>


### [6] [HiRA-CAM: Preserving Fine-Grained Spatial Relevance in Gradient-Based Visual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19407)
*Manasi Nerurkar,Ali A. Minai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为HiRA-CAM的改进方法，用于增强卷积神经网络（CNN）的可解释性。该方法基于LayerCAM构建，通过自适应利用CNN所有层的激活图来生成更聚焦的显著性图，在目标分类任务上优于LayerCAM和Grad-CAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型可能包含数十亿甚至更多参数，内部变换和输出难以解释。然而随着AI在关键应用中的使用，可解释性变得越来越重要，需要更好的CNN可解释方法来生成有用的显著性图。

Method: 在流行的基于梯度的LayerCAM方法基础上提出HiRA-CAM，其核心创新在于自适应地利用CNN所有层的激活图，将来自不同层的信息融合，从而生成更加聚焦和精确的显著性图。

Result: 在目标分类任务上，HiRA-CAM在生成有用的显著性图方面优于LayerCAM和Grad-CAM，展现出更好的可解释性能。

Conclusion: HiRA-CAM通过自适应整合CNN各层激活图，有效提升了CNN的可解释性，为目标分类提供了更清晰的显著性可视化。

Abstract: Deep Learning models can include billions of parameters or more, making it difficult to explain their internal transformations and outputs. However, explainability is increasing in importance due to the use of AI in crucial applications. This paper focuses on the interpretability of convolutional neural networks (CNNs). Building on the popular gradient based method LayerCAM for extracting internal features in CNNs, we propose an improved method named HiRA-CAM, and show that it outperforms both LayerCAM and Grad-CAM on creating useful saliency maps for object classification. The main feature of HiRA-CAM is its adaptive use of activation maps from all the layers of the CNN to arrive at a more focused saliency map.

</details>


### [7] [VideoRun2D Demo: Markerless Body Tracking for Biomechanical Analysis of Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19480)
*Luis F. Gomez,Julian Fierrez,Roberto Daza,Ruben Tolosana,Aythami Morales,Gonzalo Garrido,Javier Rueda,Enrique Navarro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VideoRun2D Demo框架，利用多种人体姿态估计器对短跑运动进行生物力学分析，重点关注髋关节和膝关节的屈曲/伸展角度。基于44名专业跑步者的314次短跑数据，结合后处理异常值检测模块，最优跟踪器的平均均方根误差从11.46°/5.83°降低到9.87°/5.30°，证明了人体姿态跟踪方法在跑步生物力学分析中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体姿态估计技术已在深度学习推动下取得显著进展，具有体育分析和性能评估的广阔应用前景，但需要验证其在短跑生物力学分析中的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 使用多种人体姿态估计器构建VideoRun2D Demo框架，对短跑视频中的髋关节和膝关节屈曲/伸展角度进行生物力学分析，并集成后处理模块用于异常值检测。框架使用44名专业跑步者的314次短跑数据，通过人体姿态跟踪器输出与专家手动标注结果对比评估。

Result: 在测试中，最优跟踪器的平均均方根误差范围为11.46°至5.83°；集成后处理模块后，误差分别降低至9.87°和5.30°。

Conclusion: 人体姿态跟踪方法能够作为跑步生物力学分析的有效工具，为运动表现评估提供有价值的参考资源。

Abstract: Human pose estimation has advanced significantly due to the development of deep learning models, increased data availability, and improved computing resources. These developments have led to highly accurate body tracking systems with direct applications in sports analysis and performance evaluation. The VideoRun2D Demo performs a biomechanical analysis during sprints using different human pose estimators. The proposed framework was evaluated using human pose trackers and expert manual annotations. The tested framework uses 314 sprints from 44 professional runners, focusing on two key joint angles in sprint biomechanics: 1) hip flexion/extension and 2) knee flexion/extension. The framework also includes a post-processing module for outlier detection. The tested results demonstrate that the average root-mean-square errors range from 11.46° to 5.83° for the best trackers. When integrated with the post-processing modules, these errors can be reduced to 9.87° and 5.30°, respectively. The VideoRun2D Demo findings suggest that human pose-tracking approaches can be valuable resources for the biomechanical analysis of running.

</details>


### [8] [A Plug-in Interpretation of Conditioning in Score-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19504)
*Libo Chen,Souvik Ghosh,Teo Deveney,Chris Budd,Vinay P. Namboodiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多速度联合扩散的条件扩散模型框架，通过无条件的联合得分网络加插入式校正项实现条件生成，并引入log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化以缩小ODE与SDE采样之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件扩散模型要么需要重新训练条件模型，要么依赖分类器引导等启发式方法，缺乏对条件如何影响生成过程的透明理解。作者希望提出一种机制，使条件对目标分布生成的引导方式清晰可见，同时提供原理性且可直接比较的条件采样器。

Method: 1) 提出多速度联合扩散机制，同时扩散目标与条件，并学习无条件联合得分网络；2) 在推理时通过插入式校正项强制条件约束，该校正项将条件贡献从学习的无条件动态中分离出来，提供透明的条件引导视角；3) 推导显式的条件反向随机微分方程（SDE）和近似的概率流常微分方程（ODE）；4) 引入log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化以减小ODE-SDE差距，提升确定性ODE采样质量。

Result: 在条件图像生成任务上取得具有竞争力的性能，验证了插入式条件视角的有效性。额外的ODE-SDE对比实验表明，log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化能有效改善确定性ODE采样的质量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新的条件扩散建模思路，通过多速度联合扩散和插入式校正项实现透明、原理性的条件生成，并借助log-Fokker-Planck残差正则化提升ODE采样性能，在条件图像生成上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We propose a conditioning mechanism for diffusion models based on multi-speed joint diffusion of the target and the condition. The mechanism learns an unconditional joint score network and enforces conditioning at inference via a plug-in correction term. The plug-in term separates the conditioning contribution from the learned unconditional dynamics, offering a transparent view of how the condition steers generation of the target distribution. Building on this, we derive explicit conditional reverse-time SDEs and approximate probability-flow ODEs, enabling principled and directly comparable conditional samplers. To reduce the induced ODE--SDE discrepancy, we introduce a log-Fokker--Planck residual regularization that improves ODE sampling quality. Experiments on conditional image generation tasks demonstrate competitive performance and support the effectiveness of the plug-in conditioning view. Additional ODE--SDE comparison experiments show that the log-Fokker--Planck residual regularization improves deterministic ODE sampling.

</details>


### [9] [CVSD-Reg: Cross-Modal Visual Semantic Prior Distillation for Robust LiDAR Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19536)
*Eunsoo Im,Junghun Suh,Gyeonggwan Lee,Seunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVSD-Reg是一种鲁棒的全局LiDAR点云配准框架，通过从视觉基础模型（DINOv2）中蒸馏视觉语义先验到LiDAR表示中，实现跨传感器、跨密度、跨视角的稳定配准。在KITTI、nuScenes和HeLiPR基准上分别达到97.7%、99.0%和99.3%的严格成功率（SR@0.5m/1°），超越最先进几何方法最多44.0个百分点，且推理时无需相机输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的全局点云配准方法高度依赖几何表示，对点密度、扫描模式、视角和传感器特性的变化非常敏感，缺乏跨传感器的泛化能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) Stage 1通过对比蒸馏和球面流形对齐，让Point Transformer V3学生网络从冻结的DINOv2教师网络中学习视觉语义先验，保留教师嵌入空间的超球面几何；同时利用自监督InfoNCE一致性约束和软SE(3)不变性增强对视角的鲁棒性。(2) Stage 2通过对应关系学习、密度感知点丢弃增强和端到端位姿优化，将蒸馏得到的表示适配到配准任务。

Result: 单一检查点即可同时适用于单传感器和零样本跨传感器场景；在KITTI、nuScenes和HeLiPR上严格成功率分别达97.7%、99.0%和99.3%，在稀疏16线Velodyne扫描上达97.3%，比SOTA几何方法最高提升44.0个百分点，且无需相机输入或后处理ICP精化。

Conclusion: 通过视觉语义先验蒸馏，LiDAR配准可以突破纯几何表示的限制，在跨传感器、跨密度等复杂场景下实现高度鲁棒的全局配准，且推理阶段完全不依赖相机，展示了视觉-激光雷达跨模态知识迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Learning-based global point cloud registration has achieved remarkable progress, yet its reliance on geometric representations makes existing methods sensitive to variations in point density, scan pattern, viewpoint, and sensor characteristics. We propose CVSD-Reg, a robust global LiDAR registration framework that distills visual semantic priors from a vision foundation model into LiDAR representations. In Stage 1, a Point Transformer V3 student learns from a frozen DINOv2 teacher through contrastive distillation and spherical-manifold alignment, which preserves the hyperspherical geometry of the teacher embedding space. Self-supervised InfoNCE consistency and soft $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ invariance further encourage viewpoint-robust descriptors. In Stage 2, the distilled representation is adapted to registration through correspondence learning, density-aware point-dropout augmentation, and end-to-end pose optimization. With a single checkpoint, CVSD-Reg generalizes to both single-sensor and zero-shot cross-sensor scenarios without sensor-specific adaptation and remains entirely camera-free at inference. On KITTI, nuScenes, and HeLiPR, CVSD-Reg achieves strict success rate (SR@0.5\,m/$1^\circ$) of 97.7$\%$, 99.0$\%$, and 99.3$\%$, respectively, including 97.3$\%$ on sparse 16-beam Velodyne scans. It outperforms state-of-the-art geometric registration methods by up to 44.0 percentage points without requiring camera inputs or post-hoc ICP refinement.

</details>


### [10] [Where Grounding Accuracy Lives on the IoU Curve: Label-Free Inference-Time Boundary Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19553)
*Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出标签自由精度细化（LFPR）方法，利用冻结的直接问答模型自身预测来分配一次额外的高分辨率局部观察，在不访问目标标注的情况下提升指代表达式的边界框精度。在四个数据集及多个证据等级上验证了该方法在mAcc、Acc@0.5等指标上的稳定提升，同时揭示了指代选择与边界精度部分可分离，不同组件对IoU曲线不同区域产生相反方向的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型虽然能识别正确指代对象，但往往返回不精确的边界框。现有方法依赖目标标注或需要额外的训练，本文希望在推理期利用冻结模型自身的预测能力，以标签无关的方式将一次额外局部观察分配到最需要精化的区域，从而在不增加标注负担的情况下提升定位精度。

Method: LFPR方法包含四个阶段：(1)将模型预测的小区域路由到更高分辨率的处理通道；(2)在上下文裁剪中重新对指代表达式进行grounding；(3)仅在固定的几何约束（guards）下接受候选框；(4)返回坐标方向的固定中点作为最终预测。该方法完全基于冻结的"直接回答"模型，无需任何目标标注或微调。

Result: 在31,921条Ref-L4表达式上，LFPR将mAcc从72.947%提升至76.013%，Acc@0.9从55.788%提升至61.142%。在30,969条RefCOCO系列表达式上，所有数据集在Acc@0.5、mAcc和平均IoU上均有提升（合并mAcc+0.645，Acc@0.5+0.817）。在Flickr30K Entities前瞻性评估中所有端点均提升（mAcc+0.973，Acc@0.9+1.022），单框变体提升更大（mAcc+2.575，Acc@0.9+3.689）。对两个发布的grounding专家模型应用同样算子，Acc@0.9分别提升+1.569和+6.716。未加guards的控制实验在所有指标上均劣于原方法。

Conclusion: 指代选择与边界精度是部分可分离的能力，LFPR通过一次标签无关的局部精化观察即可稳定提升精度。不同组件（路由vs.裁剪/guards/融合）对IoU曲线的不同区域产生相反方向的影响，这种异构效应是单一阈值指标无法揭示的。LFPR可以组合而非替代专家模型的专门训练。

Abstract: Vision--language models can identify the correct referent while returning an imprecise bounding box. We study whether a frozen direct-answer model can use its own prediction to allocate one additional localized observation without accessing target annotations at inference. Label-free precision refinement (LFPR) routes predicted-small regions to a higher-resolution pass, re-grounds the expression inside a context crop, admits a candidate only under fixed geometric guards, and returns a fixed coordinate-wise midpoint. We report results across three evidence tiers. On 31,921 retrospective Ref-L4 expressions, LFPR raises mAcc$_{0.5:0.95}$ from 72.947\% to 76.013\% (Acc@0.5 88.531\%$\to$89.725\%, Acc@0.9 55.788\%$\to$61.142\%). A frozen transfer to 30,969 RefCOCO/RefCOCO+/RefCOCOg expressions improves every dataset at Acc@0.5, mAcc, and mean IoU (pooled mAcc $+0.645$, Acc@0.5 $+0.817$), while Acc@0.9 is unchanged overall: routing alone gains $+1.162$ points there, but crop, guards, and fusion give back $-1.192$, offsetting rather than showing no strict-IoU effect. A prospective, image-disjoint Flickr30K Entities evaluation improves every endpoint (mAcc $+0.973$, Acc@0.9 $+1.022$), more strongly under a single-box variant (mAcc $+2.575$, Acc@0.9 $+3.689$). The same operator applied to two released grounding specialists improves every endpoint (Acc@0.9 $+1.569$/$+6.716$ for EGM-4B/8B) at roughly twice the latency, composing with specialist training rather than replacing it. A genuine unguarded control (guard removed from the same candidates) underperforms the incumbent on every metric, showing the guard is load-bearing. Together, these results show that referent selection and boundary precision are partially separable, with different components moving opposing regions of the IoU curve -- behavior a single threshold cannot reveal.

</details>


### [11] [Stream4D: 4D-Consistency for Streaming Autoregressive Diffusion Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19556)
*Yuanhao Ban,Jiaqi Feng,Hengguang Zhou,Xiaohuan Pei,Justin Cui,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream4D 提出了一种用于流式自回归扩散视频生成模型的训练方法，通过引入前馈4D重建奖励和运动先验来替代静态3D重建奖励，解决长程视频生成中几何漂移和动态场景建模问题，提升4D重建质量和运动保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式自回归扩散模型支持实时长程视频生成，但其训练目标仅优化局部帧预测，忽略了世界几何与动态一致性，导致长程生成中几何漂移、生成退化为静态或不自然运动。现有双向方法使用基于3D高斯溅射重建的奖励信号，但其单一刚性3D重建无法建模动态场景，会将真实的物体运动误判为重建误差，导致模型通过冻结视频来取巧最大化奖励，这在自回归设置下尤其有害（前一个chunk的静态配置会被后续chunk传播）。

Method: Stream4D 用前馈4D重建奖励替换静态批评器，显式建模场景动态，使连贯运动获得高一致性奖励。同时引入运动先验，奖励自然的场景流幅度并惩罚抖动和非刚性伪影。最终将这两项与轻量级感知锚定项组合，形成完整的训练目标。

Result: 在多种自回归视频骨干网络和不同生成时长下，Stream4D 改善了4D重建质量，更有效地保持运动，并取得更高的与人类对齐的偏好评分。

Conclusion: Stream4D 通过动态感知的4D重建奖励与运动先验的组合，有效克服了静态重建奖励导致的静态捷径问题，显著提升了自回归视频生成中动态场景的几何一致性和运动质量。

Abstract: Streaming autoregressive diffusion models enable real-time, long-horizon video generation, but their training objectives optimize local frame prediction rather than the geometry and dynamics of a coherent world: long rollouts accumulate geometric drift and degrade into static or unnatural motion. Recent bidirectional approaches address this problem using rewards signals built upon 3D Gaussian-Splatting reconstruction. However, a single rigid 3d reconstruction cannot model a dynamic scene, so this critic penalizes genuine object motion as reconstruction error and is maximized by freezing the video. This shortcut is especially detrimental in the AR setting, where each chunk can propagate an already-static configuration. In this work, we propose Stream4D, which replaces the static critic with a feed-forward 4D reconstruction reward that explicitly models scene dynamics, allowing coherent motion to receive high consistency rewards. To further guide motion magnitude and quality, we add a motion prior that rewards natural scene-flow magnitude while penalizing jitter and non-rigid artifacts. Our final recipe combines these two terms with a lightweight perceptual anchor. Across various autoregressive video backbones and various generation horizons, Stream4D improves 4D reconstruction quality, preserves motion more effectively, and achieves higher human-aligned preference. Project page: https://banyuanhao.github.io/Stream4D/

</details>


### [12] [Block3D: Efficient Text-to-3D Generation via Block-Wise Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19567)
*Bowen Cui,Weijie Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Mingda Lin,Haoyu Zhao,Yuanyu He,Donny Y. Chen,Feng Chen,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Block3D提出了一种分块扩散框架，将离散形状标记序列划分为连续块，自回归地生成每个块并对块内所有标记进行联合去噪，配合置信度引导的块内修正机制减少误差累积，在保持几何保真度的同时实现5.15倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D生成方法存在明显缺陷：自回归解码是顺序性的且无法修正错误；扩散和流匹配模型反复处理完整表征，导致高质量生成的推理成本高昂。如何在低推理成本下实现高几何保真度的文本到3D生成是一个亟待解决的挑战。

Method: 提出Block3D，一种块级扩散框架：1) 将离散形状标记序列划分为连续的块；2) 以自回归方式逐个生成块；3) 对当前块内的所有标记进行联合去噪；4) 引入置信度引导的块内修正机制，在每块最终确定前修正低置信度标记，以缓解误差累积问题。

Result: 在TRELLIS-500K的测试集上，Block3D将平均端到端生成时间从25.71秒降至4.99秒，相比微调后的自回归基线实现了5.15倍的加速，同时不牺牲几何保真度。

Conclusion: Block3D通过结合自回归生成的顺序性与扩散模型的并行去噪能力，在保持高几何保真度的同时大幅降低了推理成本，为高效文本到3D生成提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: While text-to-3D generation has advanced rapidly, achieving high geometric fidelity at low inference cost remains challenging. Existing text-to-3D methods either decode discrete shape tokens autoregressively or iteratively refine global 3D representations with diffusion or flow models. However, autoregressive decoding is sequential and cannot revise errors, whereas diffusion and flow-matching models repeatedly process the full representation, making high-quality generation increasingly expensive. In this paper, we propose Block3D, a block-wise diffusion framework that partitions the discrete shape-token sequence into contiguous blocks, generates the blocks autoregressively, and jointly denoises all tokens within the current block. To alleviate error accumulation, we introduce confidence-guided intra-block correction, which revises low-confidence tokens before each block is finalized. On a held-out set from TRELLIS-500K, Block3D reduces mean end-to-end generation time from 25.71 seconds to 4.99 seconds, achieving a $5.15\times$ speedup over the fine-tuned autoregressive baseline without sacrificing geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [13] [Mix&Fix-Net: A Dual-Stage Trajectory Prediction Model for AIS and Vision-Derived Vessel Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19580)
*Md Mahmuddun Nabi Murad,Bora San Turgut,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Mix&Fix-Net，一种双阶段混合器架构的船舶轨迹预测模型，能够同时处理AIS数据和非AIS（视频视觉）数据，并引入了一个基于网络摄像头流的新视频数据集来代表非AIS数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有船舶轨迹预测模型依赖AIS数据，但小型船舶大多不配备AIS系统，导致监控盲区。需要一种能够同时处理AIS和非AIS数据的轨迹预测模型。

Method: 采用双阶段混合器（mixer）架构，集成主轨迹预测器（Primary Trajectory Predictor）和残差轨迹调整器（Residual Trajectory Adjuster）；引入基于网络摄像头流提取船舶轨迹的新视频数据集以代表非AIS数据。

Result: 在AIS和非AIS数据集上，使用六种指标（MSE、MAE、SMAPE、FDE、Frechet距离、平均欧氏距离）进行评估，Mix&Fix-Net在大多数指标和数据集上优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: Mix&Fix-Net有效弥合了AIS与非AIS数据之间的轨迹预测差距，为缺乏AIS的小型船舶监控提供了可行的预测方案。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction is critical for maritime safety and accident prevention. While most existing trajectory prediction models rely on Automatic Identification System (AIS) data due to its precision and availability, small vessels mostly operate without AIS, resulting in a significant monitoring gap. To address this, we propose Mix&Fix-Net, a dual-stage mixer-based trajectory prediction model designed to handle vessel trajectory time-series data derived from both AIS and (non-AIS) vision data. Our architecture integrates a Primary Trajectory Predictor with a Residual Trajectory Adjuster, enabling more refined trajectory prediction. Additionally, we introduce a new video-based dataset derived from webcam streams, from which vessel trajectories are extracted to represent non-AIS data. Extensive evaluations on both AIS and non-AIS datasets across six metrics (mean squared error, mean absolute error, symmetric mean absolute percentage error, final displacement error, Frechet distance, and average Euclidean distance) demonstrate that Mix&Fix-Net consistently outperforms existing baselines across most metrics and datasets.

</details>


### [14] [VGI-BENCH: Probing Visual Intelligence in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19583)
*Xuan He,Cong Wei,Yuhao Cheng,Linrui Ma,Yuxuan Zhang,Zuojun Li,Yuhao Wen,Zeyi Liu,Yuren Hao,Songcheng Cai,Keming Wu,Penghui Du,Kai Zou,Rui Yang,Chenkai Sun,Ke Yang,Ping Nie,Kelsey R Allen,Chenglong Wang,Michel Galley,Jianfeng Gao,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VGI-bench基准，包含27个任务和810个实例，用于评估视频生成模型的视觉推理能力。评估显示当前最强的视频生成模型Seedance 2.0仅达到51.0%的准确率，表明现有生成系统虽然能解决部分基于视觉的推理任务，但远未达到可靠水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型展现出一定的零样本视觉推理能力，但现有评估方法存在三个关键问题：基准输入与当前视频模型的视觉先验不对齐、要求合理的演化过程而非仅合理的最终状态、以及任务难度校准不足。需要一个可靠、系统的评估基准。

Method: 作者构建了VGI-bench，包含27个任务和810个实例，采用两层级分类体系（任务领域和技能标签）进行细粒度评估。评估框架还包含多个分析维度：输出失败模式、输入条件敏感性、合成微调的性能迁移边界，以及内部去噪过程分析。

Result: 最强模型Seedance 2.0在评估标准下仅达到51.0%的准确率。内部去噪分析揭示模型具备有限的自校正能力——后续去噪步骤主要是在细化早期假设，而非纠正推理错误。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型在视觉推理任务上仍不够可靠，VGI-bench为下一代视频生成模型的发展提供了系统评估工具，作者将公开发布代码和数据以促进研究。

Abstract: Recent studies suggest that video generation models can exhibit certain forms of zero-shot visual reasoning through generated frames. Yet reliable evaluation remains challenging: benchmarks should adopt inputs aligned with the visual priors of current video models, require valid evolving processes rather than only plausible final states, and calibrate task difficulty to remain challenging yet partly feasible. To this end, we introduce VGI-bench, containing 27 tasks and 810 instances, organized by a two-level taxonomy of task domains and skill tags for fine-grained evaluation of visual reasoning capabilities of video generation models. Our evaluations show that current generative systems can solve a subset of visually grounded reasoning tasks, but remain far from reliable, with even the strongest model, Seedance~2.0, achieving only 51.0% under our evaluation criteria. Our analysis further explore the output failure modes, input condition sensitivity, performance transfer boundary from synthetic fine-tuning, and internal denoising perspective revealing limited self-correction, where later steps mainly refine early hypotheses rather than correct reasoning errors. We hope VGI-bench will help stimulate the development of next-generation video generation models. We will release our code and data.

</details>


### [15] [PEA-DPO: Perception-Enhanced Alignment Direct Preference Optimization for MLLMs Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19598)
*Jiawei Feng,Jiancan Wu,Xingyu Zhu,Junkang Wu,Xiang Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PEA-DPO框架,通过显式利用视觉偏好信号解决多模态偏好优化中的"视觉不敏感"问题,理论证明和实验验证均表明其能显著增强模型对视觉上下文的敏感度并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好优化(DPO)在文本对齐中效果显著,但其在多模态场景下的适配尚待探索。通过表征分析发现多模态偏好优化存在关键缺陷——视觉不敏感,即模型无法区分正常图像与被移除关键视觉上下文的图像,这严重制约了多模态对齐效果。

Method: 首先通过表征分析识别"视觉不敏感"问题,并从理论上将其分解为跨图像不敏感(Across-Image Insensitivity)和图像内不敏感(Within-Image Insensitivity)两种具体表现。随后提出PEA-DPO框架,在多模态LLM对齐中显式利用视觉偏好信号来克服这两种不敏感模式,并提供理论分析证明其可可靠地缓解两类失败模式。

Result: 在三个幻觉基准上使用不同规模的多模态LLM进行实验,PEA-DPO在增强视觉上下文敏感性的同时保留了基础模型的语言建模能力,有效缓解了视觉不敏感问题,实现了更强的多模态对齐并大幅减少了幻觉。

Conclusion: PEA-DPO通过显式整合视觉偏好信号,从根本上解决了多模态偏好优化中的视觉不敏感问题,理论和实验双重验证了其有效性,为多模态LLM对齐提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as an effective approach for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, its adaptation to multimodal settings remains unexplored. Through representational analysis, we identify a key limitation in multimodal preference optimization, which we term visual insensitivity: models often fail to distinguish between images and those with critical visual context removed. Our theoretical analysis further uncovers two manifestations of this problem, namely Across-Image Insensitivity and Within-Image Insensitivity. To address these challenges, we propose Perception-Enhanced Alignment DPO (PEA-DPO), a framework for multimodal LLMs alignment, which explicitly leverages visual preference signals to overcome visual insensitivity. We further provide a theoretical analysis demonstrating that PEA-DPO provably mitigates both failure modes. Empirical results demonstrate that PEA-DPO enhances sensitivity to visual context while preserving the language modeling capacity of the base model. Evaluations across three hallucination benchmarks using MLLMs of varying scales show that PEA-DPO effectively mitigates visual insensitivity, achieves stronger multimodal alignment, and substantially reduces hallucinations.

</details>
